		"hud_damage_rules_title" "Damage types & affinity"
		"hud_damage_rules_lead" "A Physical attack with Fire affinity still deals Physical damage."
		"hud_damage_rules_attribute_title" "What does affinity do?"
		"hud_damage_rules_attribute" "Attack shows the damage type of normal attacks. Affinity lets that damage benefit from another type’s damage and critical damage bonuses too. For example, Fire affinity lets a Physical attack use both Physical and Fire bonuses, without dealing an extra Fire hit."
		"hud_damage_rules_multiplier_title" "How do damage bonuses add up?"
		"hud_damage_rules_multiplier" "Matching bonuses add first: Physical damage +20% and Fire damage +30% give +50% damage. Different kinds of bonuses then multiply. Each type’s Total damage bonus is another separate multiplier."
		"hud_damage_rules_critical_title" "How often do you crit, and for how much?"
		"hud_damage_rules_critical" "Critical chance determines whether a hit crits. Only the hit’s own damage type counts; affinity adds no critical chance. A crit normally deals 1.5 times the damage. Add normal critical damage bonuses from the damage type and affinities to 1.5, then multiply by each type’s Total critical damage bonus and other kinds of critical damage bonuses. None of these apply to a non-critical hit."
		"hud_damage_rules_defense_title" "Which resistance does the enemy use?"
		"hud_damage_rules_defense" "Only the hit’s own damage type matters. Physical damage with Fire affinity uses Physical resistance, Physical penetration and increases/reductions to Physical damage taken. The enemy’s Fire resistance and Fire damage taken bonuses have no effect. Armour and general damage reduction still apply."
		"hud_damage_rules_example_title" "Worked example: 100 Physical damage with Fire affinity"
		"hud_damage_rules_example" "1. Damage: Physical +20%, Fire +30%, Total Physical +40%.\n100 × (1 + 20% + 30%) × 1.4 = 210.\n2. Crit chance: Physical 30%, Fire 50%. This hit still has a 30% chance to crit, not 80%.\n3. Crit damage: Physical +50%, Fire +30%, Total Fire +20%.\nCrit multiplier = (1.5 + 0.5 + 0.3) × 1.2 = 2.76.\nA normal hit deals 210; a crit deals 210 × 2.76 = 579.6.\n4. Defence: the enemy has 20% Physical resistance and takes 50% less Physical damage. Multiply by 0.8 × 0.5.\n5. Armour: the enemy has 10 armour, reducing damage by 37.5%. Multiply the remaining damage by 1 ÷ (1 + 0.06 × 10) = 0.625.\nResult: a 70% chance to deal 210 × 0.8 × 0.5 × 0.625 = 52.5, or a 30% chance to crit for 579.6 × 0.8 × 0.5 × 0.625 = 144.9. Fire resistance and Fire damage taken bonuses do not count."
		"hud_damage_rules_note" "The example assumes no other bonuses, resistance penetration, armour ignore or general damage reduction. Skills that cannot crit, use another type for crits or deal pure damage follow their own descriptions."
